Credit Risk Assessment on Bonds Investment and                            債券投資及出售投資應收款項之信貸風險評估
Amounts Receivables from Disposal of Investments
     During the Year, the Group has reversed expected credit loss            於本年度，本集團已就債券投資撥回預期信貸虧損
(“ECL”) on bonds investment of approximately HK$3.0 million               （「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）約3,000,000港 元 及 就 出 售 投 資 應
and made ECL on the amounts of receivables from disposal of               收款項作出預期信貸虧損約400,000港元。
investments of approximately HK$0.4 million.


     In relation to the bonds investment, it is the current practice         就債券投資而言，董事會現時慣例為至少每半年檢
of the Board to review the financial situation of the bond-issuers        討 債 券 發 行 人 的 財 務 狀 況。就 債 券 的 任 何 收 購 或 出 售
at least semi-annually. For any acquisition or disposal of bonds,         而 言，董 事 會 於 作 出 投 資 及 撤 資 決 策 前 將 征 求 本 公 司
the Board would seek the advice of the Company’s investment               投資管理人的意見。於釐定預期信貸虧損撥備金額時，
manager before making investment and divestment decisions. In             本公司聘請獨立估值師以評估本集團所持債券的預期
determining the amount of ECL provision, the Company engaged              信貸虧損。
an independent valuer to evaluate the ECL on the bonds held by
the Group.


     In relation to the receivables from disposal of unlisted                就出售非上市投資應收款項而言，董事會現時慣例
investments, it is the current practice of the Board to review the        為 至 少 每 半 年 檢 討 債 務 人 的 財 務 狀 況。就 任 何 延 期 還
financial situation of the debtors at least semi-annually. For any        款 而 言，董 事 會 將 單 獨 審 閱 債 務 人 的 財 務 狀 況，並 要
extension of the repayment, the Board would review the financial          求 收 取 應 收 款 項 利 息 作 為 補 償。於 釐 定 預 期 信 貸 虧 損
situation of the debtors on a stand-alone basis, and request              撥 備 金 額 時，本 公 司 聘 請 獨 立 估 值 師 以 評 估 該 等 應 收

Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
     It is the Board’s intention to distribute any excess balance by         董事會擬在法例、大綱及細則許可下以股息分派任

Main features of the risk management and internal control                 風險管理及內部控制系統之主要特性
systems
     To ensure the efficient and effective operation of the business        為確保業務及營運高效且有效地經營，相關內部控
and operations, relevant internal control procedures have been set        制 程 序 已 獲 設 立，以 保 障 資 產 不 被 擅 自 挪 用 或 處 置、
up for safeguarding assets against unauthorised use or disposition,       控 制 資 本 開 支、妥 為 保 存 會 計 記 錄 及 確 保 業 務 及 發 佈

records and ensuring the reliability of financial information used        討，並會適時獲更新。
for business and publication. These procedures are monitored and
reviewed from time to time and updated where necessary.


     The processes used to identify, evaluate and manage                    本集團識別、評估及管理重大風險所用的程序概述
significant risks by the Group are summarised as follows:                 如下：


     Risk Identification                                                    識別風險
     •   Identifies risks that may potentially affect the key processes     •   識別可能對本集團業務及經營的主要程序造成
         of the Group’s business and operations.                                潛在影響的風險。


     Risk Assessment                                                        評估風險
     •   Assesses the risks identified by using the assessment              •   利用管理層制定的評估標準評估已識別風險；
         criteria developed by the management; and                              及


     •   Considers the impact on the business and the likelihood of         •   考慮對業務的影響及出現有關影響的可能性。
         their occurrence.


     Risk Response                                                          應對風險
     •   Prioritises the risks by comparing the results of the risk         •   透過比較風險評估的結果為風險排列優先次序；
         assessment; and                                                        及


     •   Determines the risk management strategies and internal             •   釐 定 風 險 管 理 策 略 及 內 部 控 制 程 序，以 預 防、
         control processes to prevent, avoid or mitigate the risks.             避免或減輕該等風險。


     Risk Monitoring and Reporting                                          監察及匯報風險
     •   Performs ongoing and periodic monitoring of the risk and           •   持 續 及 定 期 監 察 風 險，並 確 保 已 設 有 合 適 的 內
         ensures that appropriate internal control processes are in             部控制程序；
         place;


     •   Revises the risk management strategies and internal control        •   倘 情 況 出 現 任 何 重 大 變 化，則 修 訂 風 險 管 理 策
         processes in case of any significant change of situation;              略及內部控制程序；及
         and


     •   Report the results of risk monitoring to the management            •   定期向管理層及董事會匯報監察風險的結果。
         and the Board regularly.

ABOUT THE GROUP                                                         關於本集團
     Capital VC Limited, also known as “Capital VC,” along with            首都創投有限公司（ 亦稱「首都創投」，連同其附屬
its subsidiaries forming the “Group,” is a Cayman Islands-based         公 司 統 稱「本 集 團」）為 總 部 設 立 於 開 曼 群 島 之 有 限 公
company with limited liability. Its shares are publicly listed on the   司，其股份於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主
Main Board of the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the              板公開上市。
“Stock Exchange”).


     The primary focus of the Group lies in investing in both listed       本集團之主要業務為投資主要位於香港及中華人
and unlisted securities, predominantly within Hong Kong and the         民 共 和 國（「中 國」）之 上 市 及 非 上 市 證 券。截 至 二 零
People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). As of September 30,             二 五 年 九 月 三 十 日，本 集 團 的 核 心 投 資 組 合 主 要 包 括
2025, the core portfolio of the Group primarily consists of small to    中 小 型 上 市 公 司。其 維 持 多 元 化 投 資 組 合，以 涵 蓋 更
medium-sized listed companies. It upholds a diverse investment          廣 泛 行 業，其 中 包 括（ 但 不 限 於）從 事 金 融 業、消 費 產
portfolio encompassing various business sectors, including but          品及服務業、媒體、製造業及建築業等的公司。
not restricted to finance, consumer goods and services, media,
manufacturing, construction, and more.

     To achieve medium-term or long-term capital appreciation.             達致中線或長線資本增值。

Group’s initiatives in striving for sustainable growth throughout the   至 二 零 二 五 年 九 月 三 十 日 整 個 期 間（「本 年 度」）為 實

“Year”). It provides an overview of the Group’s ESG management          環境、社會及管治管理方針、管治架構、可持續發展措
approach, governance structure as well as sustainability initiatives    施 及 表 現，以 及 本 集 團 於 香 港 經 營 的 上 市 及 非 上 市 證
and performance and its investments in the listed and unlisted          券投資業務的投資情況。
securities business operating in Hong Kong.

APPROACH TO SUSTAINABILITY                                              可持續發展方針
DEVELOPMENT
     As a responsible corporate entity, the Group is dedicated to          作為一個負責任的企業實體，本集團致力於通過將
generating enduring value for stakeholders by integrating ESG           環 境、社 會 及 管 治 原 則 融 入 我 們 的 經 營 中，為 持 份 者
principles into its operations, aspiring to impact its environment      創造持久價值，以期對環境及廣大社區產生積極影響。
and the broader community positively. To be accountable to              為 了 對 所 有 持 份 者 負 責，本 集 團 盡 力 減 少 環 境 足 跡，
all stakeholders, the Group strives to reduce its environmental         將員工福祉置於首位，以及為社區多作貢獻。

opportunities, and the approval of the ESG Report. Additionally,                                ESG 報告前亦須了解及遵守最新監管要求。

                              meaningful comparison.

                              變更，或任何其他影響有意義比較的相關因素。

     Balance                  The ESG report should provide an unbiased picture of the issuer’s            REPORTING PRINCIPLES
     平衡                       performance. The report should avoid selections, omissions, or               匯報原則
                              presentation formats that may inappropriately influence a decision
                              or judgment by the report reader.
                              環境、社會及管治報告應當不偏不倚地呈報發行人的表現，避免可能
                              會不恰當地影響報告讀者決策或判斷的選擇、遺漏或呈報格式。

the Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated          須 承 擔 之 責 任」一 節 中 作 進 一 步 闡 述。根 據 香 港 會 計

     Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about               我們的目標為就綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由

from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to        並 發 出 載 有 我 們 的 意 見 之 核 數 師 報 告。按 照 我 們 協 定
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion solely to you, as    的委任條款向       閣 下（ 作 為 整 體）除 此 之 外，本 報 告 不
a body, in accordance with our agreed terms of engagement, and           作 任 何 其 他 目 的。我 們 不 就 本 報 告 的 內 容 向 任 何 其 他
for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards            人 士 負 責 或 承 擔 責 任。合 理 保 證 屬 高 水 平 之 保 證，但
or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this         不能擔保根據香港審計準則進行之審核工作總能發現
report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but           存在之重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可由欺詐或錯誤引起，
is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with            倘個別或整體於合理預期情況下可影響使用者根據該
HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.         等 綜 合 財 務 報 表 作 出 之 經 濟 決 定 時，則 被 視 為 重 大 錯

         controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control               則本公司獲得控制權：
         is achieved when the Company:


         •    has power over the investee;                                     •   可對投資對象行使權力；

         investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there               有一項或以上出現變化，則本集團會重新評
         are changes to one or more of the three elements of                   估其是否控制投資對象。
         control listed above.

         and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed                     購入或處置的附屬公司的收入及開支自本

         income from the date the Group gains control until the                公司之日止。
         date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.

        Revenue recognition                                                    收益確認
         Dividend income from investments in listed securities                 來自投資上市證券（ 包括按公允值計入損益
         including financial assets at fair value through profit or           （「按公允值計入損益」）處理之財務資產）之

         rights to receive payment have been established.                      立時予以確認。


         Interest income from financial assets is accrued on a                 來自財務資產之利息收入乃按時間基準，並
         time basis, by reference to the principal outstanding and             參照未償還本金及適用之實際利率計算，而
         at the effective interest rate applicable, which is the rate          該利率為按財務資產之預計年期將估計未
         that exactly discounts the estimated future cash receipts             來現金收入準確貼現至該資產之賬面淨值
         through the expected life of the financial asset to that              之利率。
         asset’s net carrying amount.


         Other income is recognised whenever it is received or                 其他收入於已收或應收時確認。
         receivable.


        Leases                                                                 租賃
         The Group assesses whether a contract is or contains                  本 集 團 根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號 的 定
         a lease based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at                     義於合約開始評估該合約是否屬於或包含
         inception of the contract. Such contract will not be                  租賃。除非合約的條款及條件其後出現變動，
         reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the                     否則有關合約將不予重新評估。
         contract are subsequently changed.


        The Group as a lessee                                                  本集團作為承租人
         Allocation of consideration to components of a                        將合約代價分配至各組成部分
         contract
         For a contract that contains a lease component and one                就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項額外
         or more additional lease or non-lease components, the                 租賃或非租賃組成部分之合約而言，本集團
         Group allocates the consideration in the contract to each             根據租賃組成部分之相對獨立價格及非租
         lease component on the basis of the relative stand-alone              賃組成部分之獨立價格總和將合約代價分
         price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-                 配至各租賃組成部分。

         The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for              即期應付稅項根據年內應課稅溢利計算。應
         the year. Taxable profit differs from profit (loss) before            課稅溢利因其他年度應課稅或可扣稅收支及
         tax because of income or expense that are taxable                     永遠毋須課稅或不獲扣稅項目而有別於除稅
         or deductible in other years and items that are never                 前 溢 利（ 虧 損）。本 集 團 之 即 期 稅 項 負 債 採
         taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax          用報告期末已實施或實際實施之稅率計算。
         is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
         substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.


         Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                   遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表之資產及負
         between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities                債之賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應
         in the consolidated financial statements and the                      稅基之暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負債一般就
         corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                    所有應課稅暫時差額確認，而遞延稅項資產
         taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                則一般於很可能動用可扣減暫時差額對銷
         recognised for all taxable temporary differences.                     應課稅溢利時就所有可扣減暫時差額確認。
         Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all                  倘暫時差額由初始確認一項不影響應課稅
         deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is             溢利及會計溢利且於交易時不會導致產生
         probable that taxable profits will be available against               等額的應課稅及可扣減暫時差額的交易之
         which those deductible temporary differences can be                   資產及負債（ 業務合併除外）所產生，有關遞
         utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not            延稅項資產及負債不予確認。
         recognised if the temporary difference arises from the
         initial recognition (other than in a business combination)
         of assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects
         neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit and
         at the time of the transaction does not give rise to equal
         taxable and deductible temporary differences.

        Provisions                                                             撥備
         Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                倘本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法定或推
         obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past              定責任，導致本集團將可能須就該責任付款，
         event, it is probable that the Group will be required to              而相關責任金額能可靠估計，則會確認撥備。
         settle that obligation, and a reliable estimate can be
         made of the amount of the obligation.


         The amount recognised as a provision is the best                      確認為撥備的金額乃於報告期末對結付現
         estimate of the consideration required to settle the                  有責任所需代價的最佳估計，當中已考慮涉
         present obligation at the end of the reporting period,                及責任的風險及不確定因素。倘撥備按估計
         taking into account the risks and uncertainties                       結付現有責任現金流量計量，則其賬面值為
         surrounding the obligation. When a provision is                       該等現金流量的現值（ 倘貨幣之時間價值的
         measured using the cash flows estimated to settle the                 影響屬重大）。
         present obligation, its carrying amount is the present
         value of those cash flows (where the effect of the time
         value of money is material).

         provisions of the instrument.

         arising from contracts with customers which are initially             客戶合約產生的應收貿易款項除外。收購或
         measured in accordance with HKFRS 15. Transaction                     發行財務資產及財務負債直接應佔之交易成
         costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition or            本（ 按公允值計入損益處理之財務資產或財
         issue of financial assets and financial liabilities (other            務 負 債 除 外）乃 於 初 步 確 認 時 加 入 財 務 資 產
         than financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL) are          或財務負債之公允值或自財務資產或財務

         The effective interest method is a method of calculating                實際利率法為計算財務資產或財務負債之
         the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability          攤銷成本及於有關期間分配利息收入及利
         and of allocating interest income and interest expense                  息支出之方法。實際利率為於初始確認時按
         over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is                財務資產或財務負債之預計年期或（ 倘適用）
         the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash                   較短期間準確貼現估計未來現金收入及付
         receipts and payments (including all fees and points paid               款（ 包括所支付或收取可構成實際利率部分
         or received that form an integral part of the effective                 之所有費用及點數、交易成本及其他溢價或
         interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or                  折讓）至賬面淨值之利率。
         discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset
         or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter
         period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

         Dividend income and interest income from bond                           來自債券投資及銀行存款的股息收入及利
         investments and bank deposits which are derived from                    息 收 入（ 均 源 自 本 集 團 日 常 業 務 過 程）被 呈
         the Group’s ordinary course of business are presented                   列為投資收益。
         as investment income.

        Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales                    要於法規或市場慣例設定的時限內交付資

        the time frame established by regulation or convention in

         HKFRS 9. The amount of ECL is updated at each                           於各報告日期更新，以反映信貸風險自初步

         recognition.


         Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                   全期預期信貸虧損指因有關工具於預計存

         expected to result from default events that are possible                期 後12個 月 內 可 能 發 生 的 違 約 事 件 而 導 致
         within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments                  的預期信貸虧損，是全期預期信貸虧損的一
         are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                    部分。評估乃根據本集團的歷史信貸虧損經
         experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               驗 進 行，並 就 債 務 人 特 有 的 因 素、一 般 經 濟
         debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  狀況以及對報告日期的過去事件及當前狀
         of past events and current conditions at the reporting date             況以及未來經濟狀況預測的評估進行調整。
         as well as the forecast of future economic conditions.

        (ii) Definition of default                                             (ii) 違約的定義
             For internal credit risk management, the Group                         就內部信貸風險管理而言，若有內部產
             considers an event of default occurs when                              生或從外部來源取得的資料顯示債務
             information developed internally or obtained from                      人 不 大 可 能 向 其 債 權 人（ 包 括 本 集 團）
             external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely                 悉數付款（ 不計及本集團持有的任何抵
             to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full                     押品），則本集團認為發生違約事件。
             (without taking into account any collaterals held by
             the Group).

             Irrespective of the above, the Group considers                        不論上文所述，當財務資產已逾期超過
             that default has occurred when a financial asset                      90天，則本集團均認為出現違約事件，
             is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                        除非本集團具有合理且有依據的資料
             has reasonable and supportable information to                         說明更滯後違約標準更為合適。
             demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is
             more appropriate.

         (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                (iii) 信貸減值的財務資產
             A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or                      當對財務資產預期未來現金流量具有
             more events that have a detrimental impact on the                     不利影響的一項或多項違約事件發生
             estimated future cash flows of that financial asset                   時，該 財 務 資 產 已 發 生 信 貸 減 值。財 務
             have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                     資產已發生信貸減值的證據包括有關
             credit-impaired includes observable data about the                    下列事件的可觀察數據：
             following events:

             (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or               (a)   發行人或借款人發生重大財務困
                   the borrower;                                                         難；

             (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past                 (b)   違反合約，例如拖欠或逾期事件；
                   due event;

             (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                  (c)   借款人的貸款人出於與借款人財
                   contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                        務 困 難 有 關 的 經 濟 或 合 約 原 因，
                   financial difficulty, having granted to the                           給予借款人貸款人於任何其他情
                   borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s)                           況下都不會考慮的讓步；
                   would not otherwise consider;

             (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower                       (d)   借款人很可能破產或進行其他財
                   will enter bankruptcy or other financial                              務重組；或
                   reorganisation; or

             (e)   the disappearance of an active market for that                  (e)   財務困難導致該財務資產的活躍
                   financial asset because of financial difficulties.                    市場消失。

         (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                             (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
               The measurement of ECL is a function of the                          預期信貸虧損的計量是違約概率、違約
               probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                 損 失 率（ 即 如 果 違 約 發 生 的 損 失 大 小）
               magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                     以及違約風險的函數。違約概率及違約
               the exposure at default. The assessment of the                       損失率的評估乃以歷史數據及前瞻性
               probability of default and loss given default is                     資料為基礎。預期信貸虧損的估計反映
               based on historical data and forward-looking                         以各有關發生違約的風險為權重確定
               information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                  的無偏概率加權金額。
               and probability weighted amount that is determined
               with the respective risks of default occurring as the
               weights.


               Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                     一般而言，預期信貸虧損指根據合約應
               contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                     付本集團的所有合約現金流量與預期
               in accordance with the contract and the cash flows                   本集團收取的現金流量之間的差額，而
               that the Group expects to receive, discounted                        現金流量乃按於初步確認時釐定的實
               at the effective interest rate determined at initial                 際利率進行貼現。
               recognition.

        A conversion option that will be settled by the exchange             則分類為權益工具。
        of a fixed amount of cash or another financial asset for a

         In subsequent periods, the liability component of the               分採用實際利率法以攤銷成本列賬，而衍生

         is measured at fair value with changes in fair value

         A conversion option classified as equity is determined              分類為權益的換股權乃由複合工具整體之
         by deducting the amount of the liability component from             公允值扣除負債部分之金額而釐定。此金額
         the fair value of the compound instrument as a whole.               在扣除所得稅影響後在權益確認入賬，其後
         This is recognised and included in equity, net of income            將 不 會 重 新 計 量。此 外，分 類 為 權 益 的 換 股
         tax effects, and is not subsequently remeasured. In                 權將保留在權益中，直至換股權獲行使為止，
         addition, the conversion option classified as equity will           而在此情況下，在權益中確認的結餘將轉為
         remain in equity until the conversion option is exercised,          股份溢價。倘換股權於可換股債券到期日仍
         in which case, the balance recognised in equity will be             未獲行使，則在權益中確認的結餘將轉撥至
         transferred to share premium. Where the conversion                  累 計 虧 損。在 換 股 權 獲 轉 換 或 到 期 時，不 會
         option remains unexercised at the maturity date of the              於損益內確認任何盈虧。
         convertible bonds, the balance recognised in equity will

         and derivative components in proportion to the allocation           部分。與權益部分有關之交易成本直接於權

         the equity component are charged directly to equity.                債部分之賬面值，並於可換股債券期限內按

         and amortised over the period of the convertible bonds
         using the effective interest method.

4.   KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                              4.   估計不明朗因素之主要來源
     In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which            應 用 載 列 於 附 註3之 本 集 團 會 計 政 策 時，本 公 司
     are described in Note 3, the directors of the Company are               董事須就未於其他來源明確顯示之資產及負債賬
     required to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                  面 值 作 出 判 斷、估 計 及 假 設。有 關 估 計 及 相 關 假
     about the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are           設乃根據過往經驗及其他視為相關之因素作出。
     not readily apparent from other sources. The estimates and              實際情況可能有別於該等估計。
     associated assumptions are based on historical experience
     and other factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual
     results may differ from these estimates.


     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                有 關 估 計 及 相 關 假 設 會 持 續 作 出 檢 討。對 會 計 估
     an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                 計 進 行 修 訂 時，若 修 訂 僅 影 響 該 期 間，則 修 訂 會
     recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if            在 修 訂 估 計 的 期 間 內 確 認；或 若 修 訂 影 響 到 當 期
     the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the          及未來期間，則在修訂期間及未來期間確認。
     revision and future periods if the revision affects both current
     and future periods.

27. NET ASSET VALUE PER SHARE                                          27. 每股資產淨值
    The calculation of net asset value per share is based on the         每股資產淨值乃根據本集團於二零二五年九月
    net asset value of the Group as at 30 September 2025 of              三 十 日 之 資 產 淨 值438,595,115港 元（ 二 零 二 四
    HK$438,595,115 (2024: HK$307,506,138) and on the number              年：307,506,138港 元）及 於 二 零 二 五 年 九 月 三 十
    of 450,128,249 ordinary shares of HK$0.25 each in issue as           日 之450,128,249股 每 股 面 值0.25港 元 之 已 發 行
    at 30 September 2025 (2024: 420,128,249 ordinary shares of           普 通 股（ 二 零 二 四 年：420,128,249股 每 股 面 值
    HK$0.25 each).                                                       0.25港元之普通股）計算。

32. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           32. 資本風險管理
    The Group manages its capital to ensure that it will be able        本 集 團 管 理 其 資 本 以 確 保 能 持 續 營 運，同 時 透 過
    to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       優 化 債 務 及 權 益 結 餘 以 最 大 化 股 東 回 報。本 集 團
    shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity        之整體策略較過往年度維持不變。
    balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged
    from prior year.


    The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which         本 集 團 之 資 本 架 構 包 括 債 務（ 包 括 於 附 註23披 露
    included margin financing as disclosed in Note 23 and equity        之 孖 展 融 資）及 本 公 司 股 權 持 有 人 應 佔 權 益（ 包
    attributable to equity holder of the Company, comprising            括已發行股本及儲備）。
    issued share capital and reserves.


    The directors of the Company review the capital structure           本 公 司 董 事 每 年 檢 討 資 本 架 構。在 進 行 檢 討 時，
    on an annual basis. As part of this review, the directors           董事會考慮資本成本及各類別資本之相關風險。
    consider the cost of capital and risks associated with each         根 據 董 事 之 建 議，本 集 團 將 透 過 派 付 股 息、發 行
    class of capital. Based on recommendations of the directors,        債 券、發 行 新 股 及 購 回 股 份 以 平 衡 其 整 體 資 本 架
    the Group will balance its overall capital structure through        構。
    payment of dividends, issue of debentures, new share issue
    and share buy-back.

        cash and cash equivalents deemed adequate by the                 輕現金流波動的影響。
        management to finance the Group’s operations and
        mitigate the effects of fluctuations in cash flows.

        Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that                     本集團按經常性基準按公允值計量之財
        are measured at fair value on recurring basis                       務資產的公允值
        The Group’s financial assets at FVTPL are measured                  本集團按公允值計入損益處理之財務資產
        at fair value at the end of each reporting period. The              於各報告期末按公允值計量。本公司採用下
        Company uses the following hierarchy for determining                列層級釐定及披露金融工具的公允值：
        and disclosing the fair values of financial instruments:


        Level 1 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第一級－公允值計量指相同資產及負債於活
        from quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for               躍市場之報價（ 未經調整）。
        identical assets and liabilities.


        Level 2 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第二級－公允值計量指以第一級報價以外的
        from inputs other than quoted prices included within                資產或負債之可觀察輸入數據直接（ 即按價
        Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liabilities,           格）或間接（ 即按價格推算）計算所得。
        either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived
        from prices).


        Level 3 – fair value measurements are those derived                 第三級－公允值計量指以計入並非根據可觀
        from valuation techniques that include inputs for the               察 市 場 數 據（ 不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據）的 資 產 或
        asset or liability that are not based on observable market          負債輸入數據的估值技術計算所得。
        data (unobservable inputs).

    (ii) Convertible bonds equity reserve                                 (ii) 可換股債券權益儲備
         Convertible bonds equity reserve represents the equity               可換股債券權益儲備指本集團發行之可換
         component (conversion rights) of convertible bonds                   股 債 券 的 權 益 部 分（ 轉 換 權）。可 換 股 債 券
         issued by the Group. Items included in convertible bonds             權益儲備包括的項目其後將不會重新分類
         equity reserve will not be reclassified subsequently to              至損益。有關金額已扣除交易成本。
         profit or loss. This amount is net of the transaction costs.


    (iii) Capital reduction reserve                                       (iii) 股本削減儲備
         The capital reduction reserve is derived after setting off           股本削減儲備乃經抵銷於本公司股本重組（ 於
         of the capital reduction with the accumulated losses as              二 零 一 三 年 二 月 八 日 生 效）日 期 之 股 本 削 減
         at the date of the capital reorganisation of the Company             及累計虧損後得出。
         which became effective on 8 February 2013.


    (iv) Share option reserve                                             (iv) 購股權儲備
         Share option reserve comprises the fair value of                     購股權儲備包括已授出但尚未行使的購股
         share options granted which are yet to be exercised,                 權 的 公 允 值，詳 見 綜 合 財 務 報 表 附 註3中 以
         as further explained in the accounting policy for                    股份為基礎的付款交易的會計政策。倘相關
         share-based payment transactions in Note 3 to the                    購股權獲行使，該金額將轉撥至股份溢價賬，
         consolidated financial statements. The amount will either            或倘相關購股權屆滿或被沒收，該金額將轉
         be transferred to the share premium account when                     撥至累計虧損。
         the related options are exercised, or be transferred to
         accumulated losses should the related options expire or
         be forfeited.
